```
1	import {
2	  cloneDisplaySnapshot,
3	  createDefaultDisplaySnapshot,
4	  computeDisplayProjection,
5	  applyDisplayPreferencesPatch,
6	  type ChartPoint,
7	  type DisplayFieldMaterial,
8	  type DisplayPreferences,
9	  type DisplayPreferencesPatch,
10	  type DisplaySourceAvailability,
11	  type DisplaySourceMaterial,
12	  type DisplaySnapshot,
13	  type DisplayValidationIssue,
14	  type ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot,
15	  type ReadonlyDisplayPreferences,
16	  type TableRowProjection,
17	  type ChartSeriesProjection,
18	  type ScatterProjection,
19	} from '../core';
20	import {
21	  selectAvailability,
22	  selectChartSeries,
23	  selectDisplaySnapshot,
24	  selectPreferences,
25	  selectScatterProjection,
26	  selectTable1Rows,
27	  selectTable2Rows,
28	} from '../selectors';
29	import { createDisplayState, type DisplayStateContainer } from '../state';
30	
31	export interface DisplayReader {
32	  getSnapshot(): ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot;
33	  getPreferences(): ReadonlyDisplayPreferences;
34	  getTable1Rows(): TableRowProjection[];
35	  getTable2Rows(): TableRowProjection[];
36	  getChartSeries(): ChartSeriesProjection[];
37	  getScatterProjection(): ScatterProjection;
38	  getAvailability(): DisplaySourceAvailability;
39	}
40	
41	export interface DisplayOperationResult {
42	  readonly ok: boolean;
43	  readonly issues: readonly DisplayValidationIssue[];
44	  readonly snapshot: ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot;
45	}
46	
47	export interface DisplayService extends DisplayReader {
48	  updatePreferences(patch: DisplayPreferencesPatch): DisplayOperationResult;
49	  ingestSourceMaterial(material: DisplaySourceMaterial): DisplayOperationResult;
50	  clearProjection(): DisplayOperationResult;
51	  reset(): DisplayOperationResult;
52	}
53	
54	interface DisplayBuffer {
55	  chartHistory: Map<string, ChartPoint[]>;
56	  sourceFields: DisplayFieldMaterial[];
57	}
58	
59	function recomputeSnapshot(
60	  buffer: DisplayBuffer,
61	  preferences: DisplayPreferences,
62	  availability: DisplaySourceAvailability,
63	  state: DisplayStateContainer,
64	): DisplaySnapshot {
65	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
66	  const projection = computeDisplayProjection(
67	    buffer.sourceFields,
68	    preferences,
69	    buffer.chartHistory,
70	  );
71	
72	  return state.replaceSnapshot({
73	    schemaVersion: state.getSnapshot().schemaVersion,
74	    preferences,
75	    projection,
76	    availability,
77	    lastUpdatedAt: now,
78	  });
79	}
80	
81	function toOperationResult(
82	  snapshot: DisplaySnapshot,
83	  issues: readonly DisplayValidationIssue[],
84	): DisplayOperationResult {
85	  return {
86	    ok: issues.every((i) => i.severity !== 'error'),
87	    issues,
88	    snapshot: cloneDisplaySnapshot(snapshot),
89	  };
90	}
91	
92	export function createDisplayReader(
93	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot,
94	): DisplayReader {
95	  return {
96	    getSnapshot() {
97	      return selectDisplaySnapshot(snapshotProvider());
98	    },
99	    getPreferences() {
100	      return selectPreferences(snapshotProvider());
101	    },
102	    getTable1Rows() {
103	      return selectTable1Rows(snapshotProvider());
104	    },
105	    getTable2Rows() {
106	      return selectTable2Rows(snapshotProvider());
107	    },
108	    getChartSeries() {
109	      return selectChartSeries(snapshotProvider());
110	    },
111	    getScatterProjection() {
112	      return selectScatterProjection(snapshotProvider());
113	    },
114	    getAvailability() {
115	      return selectAvailability(snapshotProvider());
116	    },
117	  };
118	}
119	
120	export function createDisplayService(
121	  state: DisplayStateContainer = createDisplayState(),
122	): DisplayService {
123	  const reader = createDisplayReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
124	
125	  const buffer: DisplayBuffer = {
126	    chartHistory: new Map(),
127	    sourceFields: [],
128	  };
129	
130	  return {
131	    ...reader,
132	
133	    updatePreferences(patch) {
134	      const current = state.getSnapshot();
135	      const result = applyDisplayPreferencesPatch(current, patch);
136	      const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
137	        buffer,
138	        result.snapshot.preferences,
139	        current.availability,
140	        state,
141	      );
142	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, result.issues);
143	    },
144	
145	    ingestSourceMaterial(material) {
146	      const current = state.getSnapshot();
147	
148	      if (material.fields) {
149	        buffer.sourceFields = material.fields.map((f) => ({ ...f }));
150	      }
151	
152	      if (material.availability) {
153	        const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
154	          buffer,
155	          current.preferences,
156	          material.availability,
157	          state,
158	        );
159	        return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
160	      }
161	
162	      const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
163	        buffer,
164	        current.preferences,
165	        current.availability,
166	        state,
167	      );
168	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
169	    },
170	
171	    clearProjection() {
172	      const current = state.getSnapshot();
173	      buffer.sourceFields = [];
174	      buffer.chartHistory.clear();
175	
176	      const snapshot = state.replaceSnapshot({
177	        ...createDefaultDisplaySnapshot(),
178	        preferences: current.preferences,
179	        availability: current.availability,
180	      });
181	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
182	    },
183	
184	    reset() {
185	      buffer.sourceFields = [];
186	      buffer.chartHistory.clear();
187	
188	      const snapshot = state.resetSnapshot();
189	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
190	    },
191	  };
192	}
193	
```

```
1	/**
2	 * T003: End-to-end task → send execution chain.
3	 *
4	 * Verifies the complete lifecycle: TaskDefinition created → taskService.startTask
5	 * → send step executes → sendService.execute called → transport write occurs →
6	 * task reaches completed state.
7	 *
8	 * Verification points:
9	 * 1. task lifecycle correctly transitions (created → running → completed)
10	 * 2. send pipeline 9-step executes correctly
11	 * 3. SendResult returned and task progress updated
12	 * 4. Selector reflects task terminal state
13	 * 5. Written bytes match expected frame structure
14	 */
15	import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
16	import { wireFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';
17	import { createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter } from '@/features/connection';
18	import type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
19	import type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';
20	import type { TaskDefinition } from '@/features/task';
21	
22	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
23	// Frame definition
24	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
25	
26	const sendFrame: FrameAsset = {
27	  id: 't003-send-frame',
28	  name: 'T003 Send Frame',
29	  direction: 'send',
30	  fields: [
31	    {
32	      id: 'header',
33	      name: 'Header',
34	      dataType: 'uint8',
35	      length: 1,
36	      inputType: 'input',
37	      configurable: false,
38	      options: [],
39	      dataParticipationType: 'direct',
40	      defaultValue: '0xAB',
41	    },
42	    {
43	      id: 'payload',
44	      name: 'Payload',
45	      dataType: 'uint16',
46	      length: 2,
47	      inputType: 'input',
48	      configurable: true,
49	      options: [],
50	      dataParticipationType: 'direct',
51	      defaultValue: '0',
52	    },
53	  ],
54	  identifierRules: [],
55	  options: { autoChecksum: false, bigEndian: true, includeLengthField: false },
56	};
57	
58	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
59	// Helpers
60	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
61	
62	function setupFeatures(): RewriteWiredFeatures {
63	  const adapter = createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter();
64	  const features = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
65	
66	  // Add frame definition
67	  features.frameService.upsertFrame(sendFrame);
68	
69	  return features;
70	}
71	
72	async function connectAndGetTarget(features: RewriteWiredFeatures): Promise<string> {
73	  const connectResult = await features.connectionService.connect({
74	    kind: 'tcp-client',
75	    id: 't003-conn',
76	    host: '127.0.0.1',
77	    port: 9999,
78	  });
79	  expect(connectResult.ok).toBe(true);
80	
81	  const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();
82	  expect(targets.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
83	
84	  return targets[0]!.targetId;
85	}
86	
87	function createImmediateSendTask(targetId: string): TaskDefinition {
88	  return {
89	    id: 't003-task',
90	    name: 'T003 Immediate Send Task',
91	    schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
92	    steps: [
93	      {
94	        kind: 'send',
95	        id: 'step-1',
96	        name: 'Send frame',
97	        config: {
98	          frameId: sendFrame.id,
99	          targetId,
100	          userFieldValues: { payload: 0x1234 },
101	        },
102	      },
103	    ],
104	    errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },
105	  };
106	}
107	
108	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
109	// Tests
110	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
111	
112	describe('T003: task → send execution chain', () => {
113	  let features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
114	
115	  beforeEach(async () => {
116	    features = setupFeatures();
117	  });
118	
119	  it('task lifecycle transitions: created → running → completed', async () => {
120	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
121	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);
122	
123	    // Create task — lifecycle should be 'created'
124	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
125	    expect(instance.lifecycle).toBe('created');
126	    expect(instance.instanceId).toBeTruthy();
127	
128	    // Start task — lifecycle should transition to 'running'
129	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
130	
131	    const runningInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
132	    expect(runningInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('running');
133	
134	    // Wait for completion
135	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
136	
137	    const completedInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
138	    expect(completedInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
139	  });
140	
141	  it('send service executes and bytes are written to transport', async () => {
142	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
143	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);
144	
145	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
146	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
147	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
148	
149	    // Verify task completed successfully
150	    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
151	    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
152	
153	    // Verify bytes were written through the fake adapter
154	    // Use the connection service to verify write was attempted
155	    const snapshot = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();
156	    expect(snapshot.runtimeFacts.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
157	  });
158	
159	  it('task progress reflects step completion', async () => {
160	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
161	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);
162	
163	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
164	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
165	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
166	
167	    const progress = features.taskService.getProgress(instance.instanceId);
168	    // Progress should exist after completion
169	    expect(progress).toBeDefined();
170	  });
171	
172	  it('task selector snapshot reflects terminal task', async () => {
173	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
174	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);
175	
176	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
177	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
178	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
179	
180	    const snapshot = features.taskService.getSnapshot();
181	    const taskInSnapshot = snapshot.instances.find(
182	      (t) => t.instanceId === instance.instanceId,
183	    );
184	    expect(taskInSnapshot).toBeDefined();
185	    expect(taskInSnapshot!.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
186	  });
187	
188	  it('send with target-unavailable results in task failure', async () => {
189	    // Do NOT connect — target won't exist
190	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask('nonexistent-target');
191	
192	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
193	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
194	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
195	
196	    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
197	    // Task should fail because target is unavailable
198	    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('failed');
199	  });
200	
201	  it('multi-step task executes steps sequentially', async () => {
202	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
203	
204	    const taskDef: TaskDefinition = {
205	      id: 't003-multi-step',
206	      name: 'T003 Multi Step Task',
207	      schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
208	      steps: [
209	        {
210	          kind: 'send',
211	          id: 'step-1',
212	          name: 'First send',
213	          config: {
214	            frameId: sendFrame.id,
215	            targetId,
216	            userFieldValues: { payload: 0x1000 },
217	          },
218	        },
219	        {
220	          kind: 'delay',
221	          id: 'step-2',
222	          name: 'Short delay',
223	          config: { durationMs: 10 },
224	        },
225	        {
226	          kind: 'send',
227	          id: 'step-3',
228	          name: 'Second send',
229	          config: {
230	            frameId: sendFrame.id,
231	            targetId,
232	            userFieldValues: { payload: 0x2000 },
233	          },
234	        },
235	      ],
236	      errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },
237	    };
238	
239	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
240	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
241	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
242	
243	    // All steps should complete without error
244	    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
245	    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
246	  });
247	
248	  it('task can be stopped while running', async () => {
249	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
250	
251	    const taskDef: TaskDefinition = {
252	      id: 't003-stoppable',
253	      name: 'T003 Stoppable Task',
254	      schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
255	      steps: [
256	        {
257	          kind: 'delay',
258	          id: 'step-1',
259	          name: 'Long delay',
260	          config: { durationMs: 10000 },
261	        },
262	        {
263	          kind: 'send',
264	          id: 'step-2',
265	          name: 'Send after delay',
266	          config: {
267	            frameId: sendFrame.id,
268	            targetId,
269	          },
270	        },
271	      ],
272	      errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },
273	    };
274	
275	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
276	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
277	
278	    // Stop immediately while delay is running
279	    features.taskService.stopTask(instance.instanceId);
280	
281	    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
282	    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('stopped');
283	  });
284	
285	  it('task can be removed after reaching terminal state', async () => {
286	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
287	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);
288	
289	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
290	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
291	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
292	
293	    expect(features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId)?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
294	
295	    // Remove completed task
296	    features.taskService.removeTask(instance.instanceId);
297	    expect(features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId)).toBeUndefined();
298	  });
299	});
300
```

```
1	import { cloneConnectionStateSnapshot, cloneTransportConfig } from './clone';
2	import {
3	  CONNECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
4	  type ConnectionCounterSnapshot,
5	  type ConnectionRuntimeFact,
6	  type ConnectionStateSnapshot,
7	  type NormalizedTransportEventInput,
8	  type ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
9	  type TransportConfig,
10	  type TransportErrorSnapshot,
11	  type TransportEventKind,
12	  type TransportEventSnapshot,
13	  type TransportTargetRole,
14	  type TransportTargetSnapshot,
15	} from './types';
16	
17	const EVENT_LIMIT = 50;
18	
19	export function createEmptyConnectionCounters(): ConnectionCounterSnapshot {
20	  return {
21	    connectAttempts: 0,
22	    successfulConnects: 0,
23	    disconnects: 0,
24	    writeAttempts: 0,
25	    writeAccepted: 0,
26	    writeFailures: 0,
27	    rxBytes: 0,
28	    txBytes: 0,
29	    errorCount: 0,
30	    staleEvents: 0,
31	  };
32	}
33	
34	export function createEmptyConnectionSnapshot(): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
35	  return {
36	    schemaVersion: CONNECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
37	    configs: [],
38	    runtimeFacts: [],
39	    events: [],
40	  };
41	}
42	
43	function targetRoleForConfig(config: TransportConfig): TransportTargetRole {
44	  switch (config.kind) {
45	    case 'serial':
46	      return 'serial-port';
47	    case 'tcp-client':
48	      return 'tcp-client-peer';
49	    case 'tcp-server':
50	      return 'tcp-server-listener';
51	    case 'udp':
52	      return 'udp-remote';
53	  }
54	}
55	
56	function routeLabelForConfig(config: TransportConfig): string {
57	  switch (config.kind) {
58	    case 'serial':
59	      return config.portPath;
60	    case 'tcp-client':
61	    case 'tcp-server':
62	      return `${config.host}:${config.port}`;
63	    case 'udp':
64	      return config.remoteHost && config.remotePort
65	        ? `${config.remoteHost}:${config.remotePort}`
66	        : `${config.localHost}:${config.localPort}`;
67	  }
68	}
69	
70	export function deriveTransportTarget(
71	  config: TransportConfig,
72	  available: boolean,
73	): TransportTargetSnapshot {
74	  const routeLabel = routeLabelForConfig(config);
75	  return {
76	    targetId: `transport:${config.id}`,
77	    connectionId: config.id,
78	    kind: config.kind,
79	    role: targetRoleForConfig(config),
80	    label: config.label ?? routeLabel,
81	    routeLabel,
82	    available,
83	  };
84	}
85	
86	export function createDisconnectedRuntimeFact(config: TransportConfig): ConnectionRuntimeFact {
87	  return {
88	    connectionId: config.id,
89	    kind: config.kind,
90	    lifecycle: 'disconnected',
91	    config: cloneTransportConfig(config),
92	    target: deriveTransportTarget(config, false),
93	    counters: createEmptyConnectionCounters(),
94	  };
95	}
96	
97	function toEventError(
98	  event: NormalizedTransportEventInput,
99	): TransportErrorSnapshot | undefined {
100	  if (!event.error) {
101	    return undefined;
102	  }
103	
104	  return {
105	    kind: event.error.kind,
106	    message: event.error.message,
107	    occurredAt: event.error.occurredAt ?? event.occurredAt,
108	    connectionId: event.error.connectionId ?? event.connectionId,
109	    ...(event.error.recoverable !== undefined ? { recoverable: event.error.recoverable } : {}),
110	  };
111	}
112	
113	export function createTransportEventSnapshot(
114	  event: NormalizedTransportEventInput,
115	): TransportEventSnapshot {
116	  const error = toEventError(event);
117	  return {
118	    id: `${event.connectionId}:${event.kind}:${event.occurredAt}`,
119	    kind: event.kind,
120	    connectionId: event.connectionId,
121	    occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
122	    ...(event.target ? { target: event.target } : {}),
123	    ...(event.byteLength !== undefined ? { byteLength: event.byteLength } : {}),
124	    ...(event.bytes ? { bytes: event.bytes } : {}),
125	    ...(error ? { error } : {}),
126	    ...(event.reconnectAttempt !== undefined ? { reconnectAttempt: event.reconnectAttempt } : {}),
127	    ...(event.reconnectNextAt ? { reconnectNextAt: event.reconnectNextAt } : {}),
128	  };
129	}
130	
131	function updateCountersForEvent(
132	  counters: ConnectionCounterSnapshot,
133	  event: TransportEventSnapshot,
134	): ConnectionCounterSnapshot {
135	  switch (event.kind) {
136	    case 'connect-requested':
137	      return { ...counters, connectAttempts: counters.connectAttempts + 1 };
138	    case 'connected':
139	      return { ...counters, successfulConnects: counters.successfulConnects + 1 };
140	    case 'disconnected':
141	      return { ...counters, disconnects: counters.disconnects + 1 };
142	    case 'data':
143	      return { ...counters, rxBytes: counters.rxBytes + (event.byteLength ?? 0) };
144	    case 'write-requested':
145	      return { ...counters, writeAttempts: counters.writeAttempts + 1 };
146	    case 'write-accepted':
147	      return {
148	        ...counters,
149	        writeAccepted: counters.writeAccepted + 1,
150	        txBytes: counters.txBytes + (event.byteLength ?? 0),
151	      };
152	    case 'write-failed':
153	      return {
154	        ...counters,
155	        writeFailures: counters.writeFailures + 1,
156	        errorCount: counters.errorCount + 1,
157	      };
158	    case 'error':
159	      return { ...counters, errorCount: counters.errorCount + 1 };
160	    case 'stale-event':
161	      return { ...counters, staleEvents: counters.staleEvents + 1 };
162	    case 'cleanup':
163	    case 'disconnect-requested':
164	    case 'reconnect-scheduled':
165	    case 'reconnect-exhausted':
166	      return counters;
167	  }
168	}
169	
170	function lifecycleForEvent(
171	  current: ConnectionRuntimeFact,
172	  eventKind: TransportEventKind,
173	): ConnectionRuntimeFact['lifecycle'] {
174	  switch (eventKind) {
175	    case 'connect-requested':
176	      return 'connecting';
177	    case 'connected':
178	      return 'connected';
179	    case 'disconnect-requested':
180	      return 'disconnecting';
181	    case 'disconnected':
182	    case 'cleanup':
183	      return 'disconnected';
184	    case 'error':
185	      return 'error';
186	    case 'write-failed':
187	    case 'data':
188	    case 'write-requested':
189	    case 'write-accepted':
190	    case 'stale-event':
191	    case 'reconnect-scheduled':
192	    case 'reconnect-exhausted':
193	      return current.lifecycle;
194	  }
195	}
196	
197	function applyEventToFact(
198	  fact: ConnectionRuntimeFact,
199	  event: TransportEventSnapshot,
200	): ConnectionRuntimeFact {
201	  const lifecycle = lifecycleForEvent(fact, event.kind);
202	  const available =
203	    lifecycle === 'connected' &&
204	    event.kind !== 'disconnected' &&
205	    event.kind !== 'cleanup' &&
206	    event.kind !== 'error';
207	  const target = event.target ?? deriveTransportTarget(fact.config, available);
208	  const counters = updateCountersForEvent(fact.counters, event);
209	
210	  const reconnectUpdate =
211	    event.kind === 'reconnect-scheduled'
212	      ? {
213	          reconnectAttempt: event.reconnectAttempt ?? fact.reconnectAttempt,
214	          reconnectNextAt: event.reconnectNextAt ?? fact.reconnectNextAt,
215	        }
216	      : event.kind === 'reconnect-exhausted'
217	        ? { reconnectAttempt: undefined, reconnectNextAt: undefined }
218	        : event.kind === 'connected' || event.kind === 'disconnected' || event.kind === 'cleanup'
219	          ? { reconnectAttempt: undefined, reconnectNextAt: undefined }
220	          : {};
221	
222	  return {
223	    ...fact,
224	    lifecycle,
225	    target: { ...target, available },
226	    counters,
227	    ...(event.kind === 'data' || event.kind === 'write-accepted'
228	      ? { lastActivityAt: event.occurredAt }
229	      : fact.lastActivityAt
230	        ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt }
231	        : {}),
232	    ...(event.error ? { lastError: event.error } : fact.lastError ? { lastError: fact.lastError } : {}),
233	    ...reconnectUpdate,
234	  };
235	}
236	
237	function appendEvent(
238	  events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[],
239	  event: TransportEventSnapshot,
240	): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
241	  return [...events, event].slice(-EVENT_LIMIT);
242	}
243	
244	export function upsertConnectionConfig(
245	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
246	  config: TransportConfig,
247	): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
248	  const current = cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
249	  const configCopy = cloneTransportConfig(config);
250	  const configs = [
251	    ...current.configs.filter((item) => item.id !== configCopy.id),
252	    configCopy,
253	  ].sort((left, right) => left.id.localeCompare(right.id));
254	
255	  const existingFact = current.runtimeFacts.find((fact) => fact.connectionId === configCopy.id);
256	  const runtimeFacts = [
257	    ...current.runtimeFacts.filter((fact) => fact.connectionId !== configCopy.id),
258	    existingFact
259	      ? {
260	          ...existingFact,
261	          kind: configCopy.kind,
262	          config: cloneTransportConfig(configCopy),
263	          target: deriveTransportTarget(configCopy, existingFact.target.available),
264	        }
265	      : createDisconnectedRuntimeFact(configCopy),
266	  ].sort((left, right) => left.connectionId.localeCompare(right.connectionId));
267	
268	  return {
269	    ...current,
270	    configs,
271	    runtimeFacts,
272	  };
273	}
274	
275	export function reduceTransportEvent(
276	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
277	  input: NormalizedTransportEventInput,
278	): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
279	  const current = cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
280	  const event = createTransportEventSnapshot(input);
281	  const fact = current.runtimeFacts.find((item) => item.connectionId === event.connectionId);
282	
283	  if (!fact) {
284	    const staleError: TransportErrorSnapshot = {
285	      kind: 'stale-event',
286	      message: `Ignored stale transport event for ${event.connectionId}.`,
287	      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
288	      connectionId: event.connectionId,
289	      recoverable: true,
290	    };
291	    const staleEvent: TransportEventSnapshot = {
292	      id: `${event.connectionId}:stale-event:${event.occurredAt}`,
293	      kind: 'stale-event',
294	      connectionId: event.connectionId,
295	      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
296	      error: staleError,
297	    };
298	    return {
299	      ...current,
300	      events: appendEvent(current.events, staleEvent),
301	      lastError: staleError,
302	    };
303	  }
304	
305	  const nextFact = applyEventToFact(fact, event);
306	  const runtimeFacts = current.runtimeFacts.map((item) =>
307	    item.connectionId === nextFact.connectionId ? nextFact : item,
308	  );
309	  const lastError = event.error ?? current.lastError;
310	
311	  return {
312	    ...current,
313	    runtimeFacts,
314	    events: appendEvent(current.events, event),
315	    ...(lastError ? { lastError } : {}),
316	  };
317	}
318
```

```
1	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';

4	
5	// --- Step kinds ---
6	
7	export const TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay'] as const;
8	export type TaskStepKind = (typeof TASK_STEP_KINDS)[number];
9	
10	// --- Comparison ---
11	
12	export const COMPARISON_OPERATORS = [
13	  'eq',
14	  'neq',
15	  'gt',
16	  'lt',
17	  'gte',
18	  'lte',
19	  'contains',
20	  'change',
21	  'any',
22	] as const;
23	export type ComparisonOperator = (typeof COMPARISON_OPERATORS)[number];
24	
25	// --- ConditionTerm (replaces WaitCondition, adds logicOperator) ---
26	
27	export interface ConditionTerm {
28	  readonly frameId: string;
29	  readonly fieldId: string;
30	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
31	  readonly threshold: string | number;
32	  readonly sourceId?: string;
33	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or'; // default 'and'; first term's logicOperator is ignored
34	}
35	
36	// --- ScheduleDriver (replaces TaskSchedulingMode + flat scheduling fields) ---
37	
38	export type ScheduleDriver =
39	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
40	  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
41	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
42	
43	// --- FieldVariation ---
44	
45	export interface FieldVariation {
46	  readonly fieldId: string;
47	  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
48	}
49	
50	// --- StepRepeat ---
51	
52	export interface StepRepeat {
53	  readonly intervalMs: number;

55	  readonly maxCount?: number;
56	}
57	
58	// --- Step definitions ---
59	
60	export interface SendStepConfig {

62	  readonly targetId: string; // required (was optional + fallback to definition.targetId)
63	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
64	  readonly variables?: VariableMap;
65	  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;
66	  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;
67	}
68	
69	export interface WaitConditionConfig {
70	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // single condition -> condition array
71	  readonly timeoutMs?: number; // optional (undefined = wait indefinitely until signal interrupt)
72	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
73	}
74	
75	export interface DelayStepConfig {

77	}
78	
79	// --- Unified TaskStepDefinition (config field name unified) ---
80	
81	export type TaskStepDefinition =
82	  | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
83	  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
84	  | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
85	
86	// --- Error policy ---
87	
88	export const TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause'] as const;
89	export type TaskErrorAction = (typeof TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS)[number];
90

93	  readonly retryCount?: number;
94	  readonly retryDelayMs?: number;
95	}
96

98	
99	export interface TaskStopCondition {
100	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
```

```
1	import type {
2	  ChartDisplayPreference,
3	  ChartPoint,
4	  ChartSeriesProjection,
5	  DisplayFieldMaterial,
6	  DisplayPreferences,
7	  DisplayProjection,
8	  ScatterDisplayPreference,
9	  ScatterProjection,
10	  ScatterSourceBinding,
11	  TableRowProjection,
12	} from './types';
13	
14	export function projectTableRows(
15	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
16	  selectedGroupId: string,
17	  selectedItems: readonly string[],
18	): TableRowProjection[] {
19	  const filtered = selectedGroupId
20	    ? fields.filter((f) => f.groupId === selectedGroupId)
21	    : fields;
22	
23	  if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
24	    return filtered.map(toRow);
25	  }
26	
27	  const itemSet = new Set(selectedItems);
28	  return filtered.filter((f) => itemSet.has(f.dataItemId)).map(toRow);
29	}
30	
31	function toRow(f: DisplayFieldMaterial): TableRowProjection {
32	  return {
33	    groupId: f.groupId,
34	    dataItemId: f.dataItemId,
35	    fieldName: f.fieldName,
36	    value: f.value,
37	    displayValue: f.displayValue,
38	    ...(f.updatedAt ? { updatedAt: f.updatedAt } : {}),
39	  };
40	}
41	
42	export function projectChartSeries(
43	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
44	  preference: ChartDisplayPreference,
45	  historyBuffer: ReadonlyMap<string, ChartPoint[]>,
46	): ChartSeriesProjection[] {
47	  const selectedItems = preference.selectedItems;
48	
49	  if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
50	    return [];
51	  }
52	
53	  const maxPoints = preference.performance.maxPoints;
54	  const result: ChartSeriesProjection[] = [];
55	
56	  for (const fieldId of selectedItems) {
57	    const field = fields.find((f) => `${f.groupId}:${f.dataItemId}` === fieldId);
58	    const history = historyBuffer.get(fieldId);
59	    const points: ChartPoint[] = history ? history.slice(-maxPoints) : [];
60	
61	    result.push({
62	      fieldId,
63	      fieldName: field?.fieldName ?? fieldId,
64	      points,
65	    });
66	  }
67	
68	  return result;
69	}
70	
71	export function projectScatter(
72	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
73	  preference: ScatterDisplayPreference,
74	): ScatterProjection {
75	  const iField = findFieldByBinding(fields, preference.iSource);
76	  const qField = findFieldByBinding(fields, preference.qSource);
77	
78	  if (!iField || !qField) {
79	    return { points: [], sampleCount: 0 };
80	  }
81	
82	  const iVal = typeof iField.value === 'number' ? iField.value : null;
83	  const qVal = typeof qField.value === 'number' ? qField.value : null;
84	
85	  if (iVal === null || qVal === null) {
86	    return { points: [], sampleCount: 0 };
87	  }
88	
89	  return {
90	    points: [{ i: iVal, q: qVal }],
91	    sampleCount: 1,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	function findFieldByBinding(
96	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
97	  binding: ScatterSourceBinding,
98	): DisplayFieldMaterial | undefined {
99	  if (!binding.groupId || !binding.dataItemId) {
100	    return undefined;
101	  }
102	  return fields.find(
103	    (f) => f.groupId === binding.groupId && f.dataItemId === binding.dataItemId,
104	  );
105	}
106	
107	export function computeDisplayProjection(
108	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
109	  preferences: DisplayPreferences,
110	  historyBuffer: ReadonlyMap<string, ChartPoint[]>,
111	): DisplayProjection {
112	  return {
113	    table1Rows: projectTableRows(fields, preferences.table1.selectedGroupId, preferences.table1.selectedItems),
114	    table2Rows: projectTableRows(fields, preferences.table2.selectedGroupId, preferences.table2.selectedItems),
115	    chartSeries: projectChartSeries(fields, preferences.chart, historyBuffer),
116	    scatter: projectScatter(fields, preferences.scatter),
117	  };
118	}
119
```

```
117	// --- Lifecycle ---

120	  'created',
121	  'running',
122	  'paused',
123	  'stopped',
124	  'completed',
125	  'failed',
126	] as const;
127	export type TaskLifecycleStatus = (typeof TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES)[number];
128	
129	export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail';
130	
131	// --- Step results ---
132	
133	export interface SendStepResult {
134	  readonly kind: 'send';
135	  readonly sendResult: SendResult;
136	}
137	
138	export interface WaitConditionStepResult {
139	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
140	  readonly matched: boolean;
141	  readonly matchedValue?: number | string;
142	  readonly timedOut: boolean;
143	}
144	
145	export interface DelayStepResult {
146	  readonly kind: 'delay';
147	  readonly completed: boolean;
148	}
149	
150	export type TaskStepResultPayload =
151	  | SendStepResult
152	  | WaitConditionStepResult
153	  | DelayStepResult;
154	
155	export interface TaskStepResult extends TaskStepResultPayload {
156	  readonly stepIndex: number;
157	  readonly iteration: number;
158	  readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction;
159	}
160	
161	// --- Instance state ---
162	
163	export interface TaskInstanceState {

165	  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
166	  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus;
167	  readonly startedAt?: string;
168	  readonly pausedAt?: string;
169	  readonly stoppedAt?: string;
170	  readonly completedAt?: string;
171	  readonly failedAt?: string;

175	  readonly error?: string;

177	
178	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
179	
180	// --- Progress ---
181	
182	export interface TaskProgress {
183	  readonly stepsTotal: number;
184	  readonly stepsCompleted: number;
185	  readonly stepsFailed: number;
186	  readonly stepsSkipped: number;
187	  readonly iterationsCompleted: number;
188	  readonly iterationsTotal: number | null;
189	  readonly elapsedMs: number;
190	  readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null;
191	  readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null;
192	}
193	
194	// --- Execution summary ---
195	
196	export interface TaskExecutionSummary {
197	  readonly kind: 'completed' | 'stopped' | 'failed';
198	  readonly summary: {
199	    readonly stepsTotal: number;
200	    readonly stepsSucceeded: number;
201	    readonly stepsFailed: number;
202	    readonly stepsSkipped: number;
203	  };
204	  readonly durationMs: number;
205	  readonly startedAt: string;
206	  readonly finishedAt: string;
207	}
208	
209	// --- Condition match input ---
210	// Decoupled from receive internals. Service layer maps from receive public API outputs.
211	
212	export interface ConditionMatchInput {
213	  readonly frameId: string;
214	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
215	  readonly sourceId?: string;
216	}
217	
218	// --- ResolvedStopCondition & resolveStopCondition ---
219	
220	export type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
221	
222	export function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
223	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
224	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
225	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
226	  }
227	  // Immediate schedule defaults to 1 iteration
228	  if (def.schedule.kind === 'immediate' && !def.stopCondition?.maxIterations) {
229	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: 1 };
230	  }
231	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
232	}
233	
```

```
1	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepResult } from './types';
2	import { resolveStopCondition } from './types';
3	
4	export function calculateProgress(instance: TaskInstanceState): TaskProgress {
5	  const stepsPerIteration = instance.definitionRef.steps.length;
6	  const { currentIteration, stepResults } = instance;
7	
8	  const currentIterResults = stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIteration);
9	
10	  let stepsCompleted = 0;
11	  let stepsFailed = 0;
12	  let stepsSkipped = 0;
13	
14	  for (const result of currentIterResults) {
15	    if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {
16	      stepsSkipped++;
17	    } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {
18	      stepsFailed++;
19	    } else {
20	      stepsCompleted++;
21	    }
22	  }
23	
24	  const iterationsCompleted = countCompletedIterations(instance, stepsPerIteration);
25	  const iterationsTotal = resolveIterationsTotal(instance);
26	  const elapsedMs = calculateElapsedMs(instance);
27	
28	  const estimatedRemainingMs = estimateRemainingMs(instance, iterationsCompleted, iterationsTotal, elapsedMs);
29	  const lastStepResult = stepResults.length > 0 ? stepResults[stepResults.length - 1] : null;
30	
31	  return {
32	    stepsTotal: stepsPerIteration,
33	    stepsCompleted,
34	    stepsFailed,
35	    stepsSkipped,
36	    iterationsCompleted,
37	    iterationsTotal,
38	    elapsedMs,
39	    estimatedRemainingMs,
40	    lastStepResult,
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	export function isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {
45	  switch (result.kind) {
46	    case 'send':
47	      return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
48	    case 'wait-condition':
49	      return !result.matched && result.timedOut;
50	    case 'delay':
51	      return !result.completed;
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	function countCompletedIterations(instance: TaskInstanceState, stepsPerIteration: number): number {
56	  const iterationIndices = new Set(instance.stepResults.map((r) => r.iteration));
57	  let count = 0;
58	  for (const iter of iterationIndices) {
59	    const iterResults = instance.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === iter);
60	    if (iterResults.length >= stepsPerIteration) {
61	      count++;
62	    }
63	  }
64	  return count;
65	}
66	
67	function resolveIterationsTotal(instance: TaskInstanceState): number | null {
68	  const resolved = resolveStopCondition(instance.definitionRef);
69	  const { schedule } = instance.definitionRef;
70	
71	  if (resolved.maxIterations !== undefined) {
72	    return resolved.maxIterations;
73	  }
74	  if (schedule.kind === 'immediate') {
75	    return 1;
76	  }
77	  return null;
78	}
79	
80	function calculateElapsedMs(instance: TaskInstanceState): number {
81	  if (!instance.startedAt) return 0;
82	  const start = new Date(instance.startedAt).getTime();
83	  const endRef = instance.completedAt ?? instance.stoppedAt ?? instance.failedAt;
84	  const end = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
85	  return Math.max(0, end - start);
86	}
87	
88	function estimateRemainingMs(
89	  instance: TaskInstanceState,
90	  iterationsCompleted: number,
91	  iterationsTotal: number | null,
92	  elapsedMs: number,
93	): number | null {
94	  if (instance.definitionRef.schedule.kind !== 'timer' || iterationsTotal === null) {
95	    return null;
96	  }
97	  if (iterationsCompleted <= 0 || iterationsTotal <= iterationsCompleted) {
98	    return null;
99	  }
100	  const avgIterMs = elapsedMs / iterationsCompleted;
101	  return Math.round(avgIterMs * (iterationsTotal - iterationsCompleted));
102	}
103	
```

Follow the exact patterns from existing tests.\n\n## Context\n\nThis is the Batch 4 of integration tests for a Quasar/Electron rewrite project. The project uses Vitest, Vue 3, Pinia.\n\n## File 1: `rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts`\n\nCovers T017 (Task error strategies: retry/skip/stop/pause) and T024b (onTimeout vs errorPolicy interaction).\n\n### Key code facts:\n\n**TaskDefinition type** (from `rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts`):\n```ts\ninterface TaskDefinition {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly name: string;\n  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];\n  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver;\n  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;\n  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];\n  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;\n}\n\ntype TaskStepDefinition =\n  | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }\n  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }\n  | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig }\n\ninterface TaskErrorPolicy {\n  readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction;  // 'retry' | 'skip-step' | 'stop' | 'pause'\n  readonly retryCount?: number;\n  readonly retryDelayMs?: number;\n}\n\ninterface WaitConditionConfig {\n  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];\n  readonly timeoutMs?: number;\n  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';\n}\n\ninterface SendStepConfig {\n  readonly frameId: string;\n  readonly targetId: string;\n  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;\n  readonly variables?: VariableMap;\n  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;\n  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;\n}\n```\n\n**Error policy behavior** (from `task-error-policy.ts`):\n- `stop`: calls `updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop')`, returns `shouldStop: true`\n- `pause`: calls `updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause')`, returns `shouldStop: true`\n- `skip-step`: returns `shouldStop: false` with `appliedPolicy: 'skip-step'`\n- `retry`: retries up to `retryCount` times. If retry succeeds, returns result with `shouldStop: false`. If exhausted, calls `updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail')`.\n\n**onTimeout interaction** (from `task-iteration-loops.ts:262-277`):\n```ts\nif (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {\n  switch 两个文件中的所有 17 个测试都通过了。

```
以下已完成工作的总结：

**文件 1: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts`**

8 个测试，涵盖 T017（任务错误策略）和 T024b（onTimeout 与 errorPolicy 的交互）：

- **T017（5 个测试）：**
  - `stop` 策略：发送失败时任务停止
  - `pause` 策略：发送失败时任务暂停
  - `skip-step` 策略：失败步骤被跳过，剩余步骤继续（delay 步骤执行，任务完成）
  - `retry` 耗尽：3 次调用（1 次原始调用 + 2 次重试），所有调用均失败，任务生命周期变为 `failed`
  - `retry` 成功：2 次调用（1 次失败 + 1 次重试成功），任务完成

- **T024b（3 个测试）：**
  - `onTimeout=continue`：超时未触发 errorPolicy，任务完成，步骤结果 `timedOut=true` 但无 `appliedPolicy`
  - `onTimeout=skip`：步骤以 `appliedPolicy='skip-step'` 跳过，任务完成，后续 delay 步骤执行
  - `onTimeout=fail`：超时触发 errorPolicy 的停止处理程序，任务停止

使用 `createFakeSendService` 和 `createFakeReceiveEventSource` 从 `@/features/task/adapters/test-exports` 导入，遵循 `ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts` 中已有的模式。

**文件 2: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/condition-and-or-short-circuit.spec.ts`**

9 个测试，涵盖 T018（条件 AND/OR 短路求值）：

- AND：在第二个条件（false）处短路，第三个及后续条件未被访问
- OR：在第二个条件（true，带 `logicOp='or'`）处短路，第三个及后续条件未被访问
- 所有 AND 为 true：无短路，所有条件均被求值
- 混合 AND/OR：从左到右求值，无优先级，OR 在 `true` 处短路
- 空条件组返回 `true`
- AND：第三个条件为 `false` 时短路，第四个条件未被访问
- OR：第三个条件为 `true` 时短路，第四个条件未被访问
- 单个条件：直接求值
- `null` 值：`evaluateSingleCondition` 返回 `false`，无崩溃，OR 正确恢复

使用 `Proxy` 包装的 `fieldValues` 来追踪哪些字段 ID 被实际访问，以证明短路行为。这与 `evaluateConditionGroup` 在 `condition-matcher.ts` 中的实际实现相匹配，其中 `conditions[i]` 在当前迭代的短路检查之前被求值。
```

```
## Summary

Two integration test files were created:

**File 1: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/drain-input-source-blocking.spec.ts`**

3 tests covering T024c (drainInputSource heavy-load behavior):
1. **1000 batch events processed without error** -- creates a mock source yielding 1000 batch events, calls `drainInputSource`, verifies all 1000 outcomes are produced (all `config-error` since no frames are registered).
2. **Completes within 5 seconds** -- measures wall-clock time for the same 1000-event drain, asserts under 5000ms to verify no hang/OOM.
3. **Counters accurate after 1000 events** -- verifies `batchCount=1000`, `byteCount=4000`, `configErrorCount=1000`, `matchedCount=0`, `unmatchedCount=0`, and per-source stats all match.

**File 2: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/display-projection.spec.ts`**

7 tests covering T024f (display projection correctness):
1. **Table filters by selectedGroupId** -- ingests 3 fields from 2 groups, selects `frame-1`, verifies 2 rows returned.
2. **Table further filters by selectedItems** -- same setup but also filters to only `field-a`, verifies 1 row.
3. **Chart projection returns series for selected items** -- selects `frame-1:field-a` in chart preferences, verifies series appears (points are empty since `chartHistory` is never written by `ingestSourceMaterial`).
4. **Scatter projection from I/Q sources** -- configures iSource/qSource bindings, ingests matching numeric fields, verifies `i=10, q=20, sampleCount=1`.
5. **Scatter returns empty when sources missing** -- bindings point to non-existent fields, verifies empty result.
6. **Projection selectors return independent copies** -- mutates returned array and objects, fetches again, verifies original unchanged (immutability contract).
7. **clearProjection resets projections but keeps preferences** -- verifies projections clear to empty while `selectedGroupId` preference survives.

Both files follow the exact patterns from existing integration tests (Vitest imports, `describe`/`it` structure, helper functions, inline comments matching the T-number convention, mutation-based immutability checks).
```

```
**文件 1：** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts`

- **T020 (3 个测试)**：并发任务执行和共享发送服务
  - “两个任务并发运行且互不干扰” -- 两个任务使用相同的 `targetId` 并行启动，都完成
  - “停止一个任务不会影响另一个任务” -- 任务 A 有一个长延迟并被停止，任务 B 立即发送并完成
  - “每个任务都获得带有正确上下文的自身步骤结果” -- 每个任务的 `stepResults` 只包含其自身条目，没有交叉污染

- **T023 (3 个测试)**：`ConnectionBackedSendWriter` 事件依赖
  - “存在 `write-accepted` 事件 -> 返回正确的 `bytesWritten`” -- 模拟返回 `byteLength: 10`，写入器返回 `bytesWritten: 10`
  - “缺少 `write-accepted` 事件 -> 返回 `bytesWritten` 0” -- 模拟返回带有空事件的 `ok: true`，写入器返回 0，没有挂起
  - “写入失败 -> 返回 `ok: false`，`bytesWritten` 0” -- 模拟返回 `ok: false`，写入器传播错误

**文件 2：** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/connection-lifecycle-boundaries.spec.ts`

- **T024 (3 个测试)**：连接生命周期状态转换边界
  - “重复连接不会创建重复的运行时事实” -- 两次使用相同配置连接，快照中仍只有 1 个事实
  - “在已断开连接上再次断开连接不会抛出错误” -- 连接，断开连接，然后再次断开连接；没有抛出异常
  - “连接快照在连接和断开连接后反映正确的生命周期” -- 验证断开连接前为“已连接”，断开连接后为“已断开连接”

- **T024d (2 个测试)**：连接断开自动暂停空缺 (已知缺陷)
  - “已知缺陷：断开连接不会自动暂停正在运行的任务” -- 任务有延迟 + 发送；在延迟期间，断开连接不会改变任务状态；任务在发送失败后停止
  - “任务发送在断开连接后失败” -- 立即发送任务，断开连接，任务因目标不可用而停止

所有测试均遵循现有的集成测试模式：`setupFeatures()` 辅助函数使用 `createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter` + `wireFeatures`，`connectAndGetTarget()` 辅助函数用于获取连接目标，以及 `errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' }`（来自类型定义的正确字段名称）。
```

```
1	/**
2	 * T024: Connection lifecycle state transition boundaries.
3	 * T024d: Connection disconnect -> active task auto-pause (known gap).
4	 *
5	 * T024 verifies connection service lifecycle state transitions,
6	 * duplicate connect handling, and disconnect edge cases.
7	 *
8	 * T024d documents a known gap: connection disconnect does NOT automatically
9	 * pause active tasks. The task only fails when it next tries to send.
10	 *
11	 * Verification points:
12	 * T024:
13	 * 1. Duplicate connect doesn't create duplicate runtime facts
14	 * 2. Disconnect on already disconnected doesn't throw
15	 * 3. Connection snapshot reflects correct lifecycle after connect/disconnect
16	 *
17	 * T024d:
18	 * 4. Known gap: disconnect does NOT auto-pause running tasks
19	 * 5. Task send fails after disconnect (target unavailable)
20	 */
21	import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
22	import { wireFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';
23	import { createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter } from '@/features/connection';
24	import type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
25	import type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from '@/runtime/feature-wiring';
26	import type { TaskDefinition } from '@/features/task';
27	
28	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
29	// Frame definition
30	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
31	
32	const sendFrame: FrameAsset = {
33	  id: 't024-send-frame',
34	  name: 'T024 Send Frame',
35	  direction: 'send',
36	  fields: [
37	    {
38	      id: 'payload',
39	      name: 'Payload',
40	      dataType: 'uint8',
41	      length: 1,
42	      inputType: 'input',
43	      configurable: true,
44	      options: [],
45	      dataParticipationType: 'direct',
46	      defaultValue: '0',
47	    },
48	  ],
49	  identifierRules: [],
50	  options: { autoChecksum: false, bigEndian: true, includeLengthField: false },
51	};
52	
53	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
54	// Helpers
55	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
56	
57	function setupFeatures(): RewriteWiredFeatures {
58	  const adapter = createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter();
59	  const features = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
60	  features.frameService.upsertFrame(sendFrame);
61	  return features;
62	}
63	
64	async function connectAndGetTarget(
65	  features: RewriteWiredFeatures,
66	  connectionId = 't024-conn',
67	): Promise<string> {
68	  const connectResult = await features.connectionService.connect({
69	    kind: 'tcp-client',
70	    id: connectionId,
71	    host: '127.0.0.1',
72	    port: 9999,
73	  });
74	  expect(connectResult.ok).toBe(true);
75	
76	  const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();
77	  expect(targets.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
78	  return targets[0]!.targetId;
79	}
80	
81	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
82	// T024: connection lifecycle state transition boundaries
83	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
84	
85	describe('T024: connection lifecycle state transition boundaries', () => {
86	  let features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
87	
88	  beforeEach(async () => {
89	    features = setupFeatures();
90	  });
91	
92	  it('duplicate connect does not create duplicate runtime facts', async () => {
93	    const config = {
94	      kind: 'tcp-client' as const,
95	      id: 't024-dup-conn',
96	      host: '127.0.0.1',
97	      port: 9999,
98	    };
99	
100	    // Connect first time
101	    const first = await features.connectionService.connect(config);
102	    expect(first.ok).toBe(true);
103	
104	    const snapshotAfterFirst = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();
105	    const factsForConn = snapshotAfterFirst.runtimeFacts.filter(
106	      (f) => f.connectionId === config.id,
107	    );
108	    expect(factsForConn.length).toBe(1);
109	
110	    // Connect again with same config
111	    const second = await features.connectionService.connect(config);
112	    expect(second.ok).toBe(true);
113	
114	    const snapshotAfterSecond = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();
115	    const factsAfterDup = snapshotAfterSecond.runtimeFacts.filter(
116	      (f) => f.connectionId === config.id,
117	    );
118	
119	    // Should still be exactly 1 runtime fact for this connectionId
120	    expect(factsAfterDup.length).toBe(1);
121	    expect(factsAfterDup[0]!.lifecycle).toBe('connected');
122	
123	    // Snapshot configs should also have exactly 1 entry
124	    const configsForId = snapshotAfterSecond.configs.filter((c) => c.id === config.id);
125	    expect(configsForId.length).toBe(1);
126	  });
127	
128	  it('disconnect on already disconnected does not throw', async () => {
129	    const connectionId = 't024-disconn';
130	
131	    // Connect then disconnect
132	    await features.connectionService.connect({
133	      kind: 'tcp-client',
134	      id: connectionId,
135	      host: '127.0.0.1',
136	      port: 9999,
137	    });
138	
139	    const disconnectResult = await features.connectionService.disconnect(connectionId);
140	    // Fake adapter should succeed
141	    expect(disconnectResult.ok).toBe(true);
142	
143	    // Disconnect again on already disconnected connection — should not throw
144	    const secondDisconnect = await features.connectionService.disconnect(connectionId);
145	    // The service processes the disconnect-requested event but adapter may return
146	    // error for non-existent connection. Either way, no exception thrown.
147	    expect(secondDisconnect).toBeDefined();
148	  });
149	
150	  it('connection snapshot reflects correct lifecycle after connect and disconnect', async () => {
151	    const connectionId = 't024-lifecycle';
152	
153	    // Before connect: no runtime facts
154	    const beforeConnect = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();
155	    expect(beforeConnect.runtimeFacts.length).toBe(0);
156	
157	    // Connect
158	    await features.connectionService.connect({
159	      kind: 'tcp-client',
160	      id: connectionId,
161	      host: '127.0.0.1',
162	      port: 9999,
163	    });
164	
165	    const afterConnect = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();
166	    const connectedFact = afterConnect.runtimeFacts.find(
167	      (f) => f.connectionId === connectionId,
168	    );
169	    expect(connectedFact).toBeDefined();
170	    expect(connectedFact!.lifecycle).toBe('connected');
171	
172	    // Disconnect
173	    await features.connectionService.disconnect(connectionId);
174	
175	    const afterDisconnect = features.connectionService.getSnapshot();
176	    const disconnectedFact = afterDisconnect.runtimeFacts.find(
177	      (f) => f.connectionId === connectionId,
178	    );
179	    expect(disconnectedFact).toBeDefined();
180	    expect(disconnectedFact!.lifecycle).toBe('disconnected');
181	  });
182	});
183	
184	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
185	// T024d: connection disconnect -> active task (known gap)
186	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
187	
188	describe('T024d: disconnect does NOT auto-pause active tasks (known gap)', () => {
189	  let features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
190	
191	  beforeEach(async () => {
192	    features = setupFeatures();
193	  });
194	
195	  it('known gap: disconnect does NOT auto-pause running task', async () => {
196	    const connectionId = 't024d-gap';
197	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features, connectionId);
198	
199	    // Task with delay (long) + send step
200	    const taskDef: TaskDefinition = {
201	      id: 't024d-gap-task',
202	      name: 'T024d Gap Task',
203	      schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
204	      steps: [
205	        {
206	          kind: 'delay',
207	          id: 'delay-1',
208	          name: 'Long delay',
209	          config: { durationMs: 200 },
210	        },
211	        {
212	          kind: 'send',
213	          id: 'send-1',
214	          name: 'Send after delay',
215	          config: {
216	            frameId: sendFrame.id,
217	            targetId,
218	          },
219	        },
220	      ],
221	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
222	    };
223	
224	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
225	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
226	
227	    // Verify task is running
228	    const runningInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
229	    expect(runningInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('running');
230	
231	    // Disconnect the connection while task is in its delay step
232	    await features.connectionService.disconnect(connectionId);
233	
234	    // Check task state immediately after disconnect:
235	    // The task is NOT auto-paused (this is the known gap).
```

```json
}
```

```
3	 * T024b: onTimeout vs errorPolicy two-layer interaction.

5	 * Verifies that TaskErrorPolicy.onFailure drives correct lifecycle outcomes
6	 * and that WaitConditionConfig.onTimeout interacts with errorPolicy as a
7	 * first-layer gate before errorPolicy is consulted.
8	 *
9	 * Verification points:
10	 * 1. stop policy: send failure → task stopped
11	 * 2. pause policy: send failure → task paused
12	 * 3. skip-step policy: failed step skipped, remaining steps still execute
13	 * 4. retry exhausted: all retries fail → task failed
14	 * 5. retry succeeds: first call fails, retry succeeds → task completed
15	 * 6. onTimeout=continue: timeout does not trigger errorPolicy, task completes
16	 * 7. onTimeout=skip: step skipped with appliedPolicy, task completes
17	 * 8. onTimeout=fail: timeout triggers errorPolicy (stop)
```
